# SD-32 Kanban (local-file)

This is the **local-file** kanban for SD-32, paired with `progress.md` (no Hermes board; per
`SD-30-class-feature-archetype-bundle/decisions.md` Decision 14a, retired 2026-08-01). Each card
is one row; cycles update the `Status` column in place and append the receipt to `progress.md`.

Cards are ordered by **claim priority**, not by epic number. The pre-Epic-5 work (Epic 5's
protective sweep) fires first because scaling Gate 2 engines over an unchecked generator is the
failure class Gate 2 depends on not existing. After the Pre-G0 phase (cards 1-2), the gate ordering
from `workflow-instruction.md §3` applies: G0 (card 3, then card 4) → G1 (card 5) → G2 (cards 6/7,
each followed by its card-8 corpus-wide run) → G3 (card 9) → Epics 1-3 (cards 10-12) → Closure
(card 13). `workflow-instruction.md §2.4` is the dispatch script for exactly this order. **Closure
fires on the Definition of Done (all four gates' AT-32-* criteria met), never on a wave budget.**

Root cause traced, not merely regenerated: `cache_gen::hand_authored_equipment`'s four per-book adapters called only `equipment_tables()`, never each book's separate `equipmod_tables()` accessor -- so every `Equipmods`-category hand-authored row (Ultimate Psionics 113 / Ultimate Combat 19 / Ultimate Intrigue 7, e.g. `up_equipmods.lst:12` "Psionic Blade") was excluded from `hand_authored_equipment.rs`'s own write path (which assumed `cache_gen::equipment_gap` covered it) AND from `equipment_gap`'s own generator (`gen_equipment_gap_tables.rs`, whose `held` skip-set is built from `equipment_resolver::hand_authored_equipment_rows()`, which does NOT filter by category and already excludes the same keys) -- two generators each assuming the other's territory covered a population neither wrote. **`decisions.md §20`: `monster_ability` owner-less-ingest, `bestiary` (2026-08-23, this entry, prepended):** the T9 monster-ability facet-widening cycle's own residual (`no_record` 1,146 for `monster_ability`, 28 for `monster`) turned out to be dominated (~95%) not by a facet-vocabulary gap but by `scripts/transcribe_monster_tables.py`'s ORPHAN pass: an ability row no monster row of its book claims was DROPPED entirely rather than shipped, on a stub-avoidance rationale (`decisions.md §44.2`) that conflates ingestion with reachability. `decisions.md §20` draws that distinction explicitly: ingestion (shape measurable, closes `no_record`) and reachability (`reach_gate.rs`-proven) are separate claims. **Mechanism change, generic, one place**: the orphan pass now SHIPS these rows with `owners: &[]` instead of dropping them (`transcribe_monster_tables.py`), applied to `bestiary` this cycle -- 180 of its 197 orphans ship this way (17 remain excluded by the pre-existing, unrelated `unscreenable`/`unmodelled_facet` screens, which now correctly run on former-orphans too). Re-derived against the pinned oracle `7f818006e371188e5717fd18d74d18a420747fc6`: `gen_book_cache beastiary` wrote exactly 180 new `data/corpus/beastiary/monster_ability/*.json` files, 0 of the 529 pre-existing touched (confirmed both by the tool's own delta report and independently by diffing `bestiary/monster_data.rs` against `git show HEAD:` for every pre-existing record's fields). `no_record`: `monster_ability` 1,146 -> 967 (-179, one unit's disposition differs by design -- a corpus-key collision in the ledger join, not a defect), bundle total 8,434 -> 8,255. `monster` kind untouched this cycle (all 28 residual units are legitimate: 21 PI-cascaded per `decisions.md §15`/`§19b`, plus 6 `.COPY=`/`.MOD` derivative-monster rows needing real inheritance-synthesis engineering -- named, not modelled, per `§15`'s own discipline). **Reachability, proven not assumed, and corrected mid-cycle**: the 180 owner-less records do NOT reach a player (`list_monster_catalog` only ever walks a monster's own `ability_keys`) -- pinned by exact key in `reach_gate.rs::UNREACHED_RECORD_FINDINGS` and `::OPEN_FINDINGS` under `("beastiary1", "monster_abilities")`, both directions tested. **A same-cycle self-correction is recorded in the code**: an early check of this claim (before `gen_book_cache` had ever been run, so the corpus JSON denominator did not yet contain the 180) misread them as *already* reaching a player; re-checked properly after the real regen and corrected before commit -- `decisions.md §17a`'s own lesson, paid and caught within the cycle rather than shipped. **A real infra fix found on the way**: `gen_book_cache.rs`'s monster-book path resolution had no way to point at the repo-local pinned oracle short of a per-book override that also (undesirably) disables its `core_essentials` cross-book fallback; it now checks the same plain `PCGEN_CORPUS_ROOT` env var every OTHER tool in this bundle already uses, checked before the deprecated `~/workspace/repos/pcgen` default. **Tests**: `bestiary::tests::every_owner_less_ability_is_a_named_and_pinned_non_reach` (new, hash-pinned exact-set test, mirrors `monster_chassis`'s facet-widening precedent), `monster_chassis::tests::the_chassis_link_resolves_in_both_directions_for_every_book` (updated: no longer requires every ability to carry an owner, still verifies every non-empty owner resolves both ways), `monster_chassis::tests::widening_the_facet_vocabulary_does_not_reclassify_any_existing_record` (re-pinned 2656->2836, RED-then-corrected), `corpus_ingest_diagnostic`/`monster_catalog` pinned counts updated (709, and the served-vs-held split). `cargo test --locked --lib`: 2,424 passed / 1 pre-existing failure (`feat_prereqs`, confirmed pre-existing via a `git checkout --`/`git apply` isolation round-trip, unrelated to this diff). `apps/desktop/src-tauri` bins: 509 passed / 9 failed, all 9 confirmed pre-existing the same way (7 `reach_gate` + 2 `class_feature_feat_bridge`, none touching `monster`/`monster_ability`/`bestiary`). `corpus_literal_sweep`/`pi_sweep_rules_tables`/`v06_corpus_trap_report --audit`: 0 new findings (1 pre-existing, unrelated `advanced_class_guide` finding, confirmed by name and book). **Not attempted this cycle, named for the next**: applying the SAME mechanism to the other 5 already-`MonsterAbilityFacet`-widened books (`bestiary_2` ~84, `bestiary_3` ~286, `bestiary_4` ~194, `inner_sea_bestiary` ~26, `inner_sea_gods` ~4 orphans) and the 9 registered-but-unrun-through-this-mechanism books -- the code change is already generic and applies to every book via the same `transcribe_monster_tables.py`/`gen_book_cache` pass; what remains per book is the SAME per-book test-pin + `reach_gate` entry work this cycle did for `bestiary`, budgeted at roughly this cycle's own size each. The `.COPY=`/`.MOD` derivative-monster synthesis (6 `monster`-kind units, `Hydra`/`Iron Cobra`/`Gug Savant`/`Magma Ooze`) is real, scoped, un-started engineering, not a config change. **`decisions.md §23b`: `Demonic Obedience` re-typed out of `class_feature` (2026-08-23, this entry, prepended), row stays `in-progress`:** re-confirmed the 42-unit premise fresh (every record's `PRE:` set is `PREDEITY`-only, naming a demon lord, corpus-wide, no exceptions) before moving anything. A 55-unit `race_trait` baseline-drift discovery, proven unrelated to this fix via a same-code idempotence check, is logged in the receipt and forwarded, not fixed here. **`decisions.md §24`: `ability`'s 576 name-PI-blocked units ingested under a Codex-generated neutral name (2026-08-23, this entry, prepended), row stays `in-progress`:** `scripts/codex_neutral_name.py` (new) derives a deterministic, PI-free identity from `(kind, book, source_file, source_line)` ONLY -- never from the original PI name -- proved by `scripts/tests/test_codex_neutral_name.py`'s `§24b`-1 test (output unchanged when the PI name is replaced) plus a signature-level check that the public functions take no name/key parameter at all. `scripts/ingest_ability.py` now ingests the 576 records `17-ability-pi-skipped.json` previously stopped on (`§15`) under this neutral name: `data.name`/`data.key` become the Codex name, `data.codex_generated_name: true` marks the rename visibly (`§24b`-3), `data.rename` records the coordinate and reason only -- never the original string (`§24b`-4, refining `§22`). `scrub_name_pi_tokens` (new) additionally redacts any OTHER raw token whose value restates the record's own original name/key (found live this cycle: a `KEY:` token repeating the row's own PI-declared identity, which the pre-`§24` blacklist-only scan never caught) -- `§24b`-2's "appears nowhere that ships" requires this, not only the identity column. Determinism proved by regenerating twice and diffing all 47,208 corpus files byte-for-byte (`ingested_at` excluded) -- zero mismatches (`§24b`-6). Zero-leak proved two ways: every one of the 576 renamed files' exact original name/key string confirmed absent from its own written file, and a full blacklist-term scan over every string in all 576 files returns zero hits. `17-ability-pi-skipped.json` reduced to coordinates only (`§24b`-2); a parallel `24-pi-name-renamed-units.json` divergence log added. `src/bin/declared_pi_shipping_audit.rs`'s standing `NAME-PI-SHIPPED` check adapted with a narrow `codex_generated_name` exception (new tests prove the exception is narrow: a record without the marker is still caught) and its `DESC-PI-SHIPPED` field check widened to accept a comma-joined `pi_field` list. **`decisions.md §20/§24`: `race` (59→0), `monster` (28→0), `class` (21→0), `race_trait` (6→0) `no_record` closed (2026-08-23, this entry, prepended), row stays `in-progress`:** new `scripts/ingest_generic_kind.py` (kind-parameterized generic verbatim ingest, mirrors `ingest_race_trait_generic.py`/`ingest_ability.py`) writes `data/corpus/<book>/<kind>_generic/*.json` for every unit `shape_ledger.py` reports `no_record` in these four kinds. Flagging the disagreement rather than silently overriding it: if reachability-shaped completeness (merging a `.MOD`/`.COPY=` row against its base record) is what `monster` closure is meant to mean, an operator ruling should say so explicitly, the way `§16`/`§17` settled the analogous `race_trait` question.** Two real defects found and fixed on the way, both proved RED→GREEN by mutation: (1) `scrub_name_pi_tokens`'s space-preserving substring check missed PCGen's own habit of concatenating a renamed record's PI name into a `DEFINE`/`BONUS` variable identifier with no separator (`RedMantisAssassinLVL`) — fixed with an additional alphanumeric-normalized substring check, found and fixed BEFORE landing via a full-corpus cross-check against `docs/work-inventory.json` names (0 leaks across all 41 renamed records, confirmed twice); (2) slug collision detection only checked slugs assigned earlier in the SAME run, so a second invocation could silently overwrite a DIFFERENT unit's pre-existing file when two distinct source lines shared identical `corpus_key` text (found live: a `class_feature` row and a genuinely-distinct `race_trait` `.MOD` row both named "Warpriest ~ Favored Class Blessings") — fixed by seeding each book's slug set from the filesystem before assigning new slugs. One pre-existing, unrelated failure found and named, not touched: **T12: `modelled_class_books()` wired to the untabled_base_class_chassis registry generically (2026-08-23, this entry, prepended) — row stays `in-progress`:** re-derived T12's own headline figures fresh (`decisions.md §17a`) and found them stale: the census's own 2,335/92-class/118-false-positive/11-large-plus-81-small figures had drifted to **2,515 evidence-code total / 2,397 real (after the SAME 118 false positives, re-confirmed by re-running `scripts/census_t12_class_feature.py`) / 93 unmodelled classes (11 large classes, now 1,332 units not 1,331 — Magus grew by one — plus 82 small classes, 1,065 units, not 81/1,004) / one brand-new class slug, Divine Scion (46 units, `inner_sea_magic`), not present in the census artifact at all** — retro-corrected (`docs/retro/events/card11-t12.jsonl`), since concurrent lanes on this branch had moved the corpus/inventory since the census cycle ran. Per `decisions.md §17`, attacked the simple tier generically rather than hand-modelling: `src/rules_core/pilot_compute/untabled_base_class_chassis.rs` (card 12, already on this branch) is a REAL `compute_pilot_base_chassis` dispatch arm computing genuine BAB/save chassis for 20 of T12's 93 classes (11 of the 11-large tier plus 9 of the small tier) — it was simply never registered in `v06_work_inventory.rs`'s `modelled_class_books()`, so the classifier reported all 20 classes `not-ingested` even though the engine already holds and computes a real chassis for them. Fix: one data-driven loop reading the registry's own 20 entries (zero new per-class code; a 21st class costs nothing once added to the chassis registry) — no hand-modelling. **Proven, not claimed:** RED→GREEN (temporarily emptied the loop via an equivalent-typed empty iterator, confirmed both new tests fail for the intended reason, reverted, confirmed GREEN); full targeted suite `cargo test --locked --bin v06_work_inventory` 318/318 (was 316; +2 new tests), full `cargo test --locked --lib` 2397/2397 (unchanged — additive only), dual-audit on this cycle's own diff `OK_NO_BUNDLE_TAGS`/`OK_NO_TOKENS`. **Live re-derive against the pinned oracle (`7f818006e371188e5717fd18d74d18a420747fc6`, unchanged) confirms the real effect, honestly, not fabricated:** re-running the full inventory (`--stdout-only`) shows T12's evidence-code population (`class_feature_of_unmodelled_corpus_class:*`) drop from 2,515 to **951** — 1,564 units left that specific evidence code — but per `decisions.md §16` this is reported as a MOVE, not a close: every one of those units now carries a more specific `not-ingested` reason (mainly `class_feature_owner_matched_by_name_but_record_not_held_by_engine`, 4,120 total across the file, and `no_explanation_id_and_no_diagnostic_names_this_feature`), because `explanation_names_class()`'s dot-segment match requires a class-specific explanation id and `untabled_base_class_chassis`'s dispatch arm only emits the generic `class_chassis.base_attack_bonus`/`base_save.*` ids (no class name segment) — confirmed by direct read of the CRB/APG/ACG dispatch arms, which have the identical shape (no per-class chassis id either; only their separate `ground_*_class_features` calls carry class-specific ids). So **zero `class_feature` units genuinely reach `grounded`/`text-complete` from this cycle** — the honest gain is at the `Kind::Class` layer only (`Kineticist`'s own class record, e.g., moves from `class_absent_from_ClassId_ALL_and_book_class_id_enums` to the more specific `class_modelled_but_no_observed_delta_on_the_rendered_snapshot`; no class reaches `grounded` there either, for the same missing-attribution reason). **Real mechanism-sized plan for the remainder, named not guessed:** the next cycle on any of these 20 (or the other 73 unmodelled) classes needs a `push_pu_class_feature_records`-shaped generic roster mechanism — the SAME pattern Pathfinder Unchained already uses (`pilot_compute/mod.rs`): one per-class-feature explanation id (`class_feature.<class>.corpus_record.<slug>`) emitted from a corpus-derived fixture (record key + granted level; no per-feature magnitude function required for the text-only majority), wired at the exact dispatch site this cycle's own registry loop touches. That is a **generic, data-driven build** (one fixture schema + one push function, reused by every class), not 93 per-class cycles — but building the fixture-extraction script and the push function to this program's fixture-check bar is real work this cycle did not have budget to also complete and prove. **11-large-tier risk reassessed, not narrowed:** still no shared mechanic across Vigilante/Medium/Psychic/Magus/Aegis/Occultist/Mesmerist/Shifter/Kineticist/Spiritualist/Psychic Warrior — each needs a genuinely new subsystem: this cycle's chassis-registration + roster-mechanism combination lowers EVERY one of their per-feature costs (text-only majority reachable via the roster, magnitude-bearing minority still needs the real subsystem), but does not eliminate the 11 separate subsystem builds. **82-small-tier: the roster mechanism is very plausibly the full closure path** for most of them (narrow, largely auto-granted feature progressions, per the census's own characterization, re-confirmed by this cycle's fresh class list) — next cycle should build the roster mechanism against 1-2 small classes first (e.g. Antipaladin or Cryptic, both already chassis-registered by this cycle) as the proof case before batching the rest. It escalated rather than working around it (guarded-path-only write scope). **Fix the ingest key collision so appended tokens survive, regenerate through the guarded path, and prove the mechanics come back.** Then re-derive how many pool groups it unblocks — cycle 7 measured **0 closable** without it. `§10`: SD-32 does not close until this row reads `complete`. **Cycle 1 update:** **Ingestion silently drops mechanical tokens -- shape (a) fixed at source, shape (b) escalated.** `class_feature::generate` (`src/rules_core/cache_gen/class_feature.rs`) read only ONE base corpus row per unit; a real PCGen `.MOD`-appended `BONUS:VAR` line targeting the SAME base name lives on a SEPARATE row and was silently dropped. Reused the ALREADY-EXISTING closure machinery (`wiring_class::token_closure_rows`/`build_mod_index`, previously wired only into `wiring_class` classification via `WiringClassIndex::wiring_class_for`) by adding `WiringClassIndex::closure_rows`/`closure_rows_for_book` and switching `raw_tokens` construction to it -- no fourth mechanism built (`§17`). Blast radius (re-derived, not trusted from the brief): a corpus-wide Python re-implementation of the SAME `build_mod_index`/`mod_base_name` resolution found **2,499 of 18,076** `class_feature` JSON records (14 books, later corrected to 18 once `.MOD` rows in nested `support/*.lst` files were counted too) losing at least one real `.MOD`-appended token pair to this collision -- an order of magnitude past the two shapes cycle 7 named. Regenerated ONLY those records via the guarded `--coordinates` path (`gen_cache_class_feature`, two passes: 2,459 then 34 more once a `source_file` basename-vs-subpath mismatch in the coordinate builder was caught and fixed) -- **2,493 files touched, matching the intended target count exactly** (staged-file count check via `git status --porcelain`). Re-ran the blast-radius query post-regen: **9 records remain**, all in `pathfinder_unchained`, all owned by a SEPARATE, deliberately-designed generator (`src/bin/ingest_pu_classes.rs`'s own `raw_tokens_excluding_bonus`, which excludes `BONUS:` tokens by explicit design, not by this defect) -- `class_feature::generate` correctly refuses to overwrite them (`foreign_citations` guard, `data.class_key` present). Escalated by coordinate, not touched (`§27b`: hard-impossibility bar -- this is a different owner's mechanism, not this cycle's to rebuild). Two more of the 43 second-pass hits were §15-correct PI redactions (`[redacted PI]`, real term "Aldori"), not losses -- confirmed via a dedicated regression test, not assumed. Stamp preservation proved BY ID: 18,076 record paths before == 18,076 after, 0 lost, 0 gained, 0 `data.key` identity changes, `wiring_class` distribution byte-identical, `license` shifted by exactly 2 (590->592 PI-REDACTED, both newly-surfaced-and-correctly-redacted tokens, verified individually). **Detector added**: two new `#[test]` regressions in `class_feature.rs` (`generate_unions_every_mod_row_s_tokens_into_raw_tokens_not_just_the_base_row`, `generate_finds_a_mod_row_in_a_nested_support_file_and_still_redacts_its_pi`) -- proved RED against the pre-fix single-row read (grafted the same tests onto the original file and ran them: both failed with the exact dropped-token signature), then proved GREEN restored against the fix; both now run on every `cargo test --lib`, so this cannot regress silently again. **Shape (b) (27/168 `class` records with no `raw_tokens` at all -- CRB/APG/ACG base classes, a DIFFERENT generator/schema than the newer `class` shape the other 141 records carry) is NOT fixed here** -- re-derived count matches the brief (141 present / 27 absent, `python3` one-off over `data/corpus/*/class/*.json`), but it is a distinct root cause (a generator that never captured `raw_tokens` for this schema, not a `.MOD` collision) and a genuinely separate, larger mechanism per `§27b`'s own hard-impossibility bar -- escalated, not guessed at. **KNOWN CONSEQUENCE for row 18**: the corpus fix flips a pinned count in `rules_core::pilot_compute::class_feature_grant_consumer::tests::the_live_scale_of_this_waves_widening_is_measured_and_pinned` from `(136, 20, 11, 9, 36)` to `(136, 21, 11, 8, 36)` (one new resolution: "summoner/Summoner ~ Bond Senses@2"). File is in row 18's forbidden-territory list -- left RED, not touched, reported here for row 18 to pick up. Pool-group re-derivation (this brief's step 4) is row 18's own resolver work and was not attempted (forbidden files). **Cycle 2 update (`t9-onboarding`, 2026-08-24, this entry, appended): all four aftermath items closed.** **1. `derived_evaluator_fixture_check::engine_evaluator_output_equals_the_corpus_derived_expected_value` (a REAL correctness bug, not a stale pin) -- FIXED, not worked around.** Live failure: `core_rulebook:class_feature:barbarian_damage_reduction` at level 7, real production resolver produced `BarbarianDR=-1`, pinned upstream `.lst` states `+1`. Root cause confirmed live: `resolve_pcgen_var_chain`'s input table (`class_feature_record_tokens()`) built `bonus_vars` via `parse_bonus_var_tokens` -- last-write-wins across every same-named `BONUS:VAR` row, now that row 21 cycle 1 restored the real multi-row `.MOD`-appended population this record carries (one ungated base formula `(BarbarianDRLVL-4)/3` plus 5 rows gated `PREVARGTEQ:...|PREVAREQ:Barbarian_CF_DamageReduction<N>,1`, a rage-power-selection offset). Fixed per the brief: **reused the existing PRE-gate-safe sibling parser** (`parse_bonus_var_tokens_pre_gate_safe`, built for the pool resolver) rather than writing a third -- `class_feature_record_tokens()` is now a thin alias for `class_feature_record_tokens_pre_gate_safe()`; the now-dead last-write-wins `parse_bonus_var_tokens` and its own now-redundant table-building duplicate were deleted (no second copy of the same logic left behind). That alone still left the target UNRESOLVED (`bonus_stack_reader::extract_addends` correctly refuses a row carrying TWO PRE-tag kinds, `PREVARGTEQ`+`PREVAREQ` together -- documented, real oracle-verified restriction, not a bug to fix) -- added **widening 3** to `parse_bonus_var_tokens_pre_gate_safe` itself: when `extract_addends` refuses a target, fall back to its own sole UNGATED row (if exactly one exists) rather than dropping the target outright. `PREVAREQ` gates on a rage-power SELECTION this module has never modelled (no binding exists to evaluate it even if a fourth parser were built), so refusing those 5 rows is correct; discarding the record's own real unconditional formula along with them was the actual defect. `cargo test --locked --test derived_evaluator_fixture_check`: 5/5 passed (was 4/5). `cargo test --locked --lib class_feature_grant_consumer` 28/28, `cargo test --locked --lib bonus_stack_reader` 21/21, `cargo test --locked --lib derived_evaluator_fixture_check` 121/121, `cargo test --locked --lib pilot_compute` 947/947 -- all green, no regression from the widening. **2. `class_feature_grant_consumer::tests::the_live_scale_of_this_waves_widening_is_measured_and_pinned` -- re-derived live, NOT moved.** The brief predicted `(136,20,11,9,36) -> (136,21,11,8,36)` (Summoner ~ Bond Senses newly resolving). Re-ran the real test against the fully-fixed corpus (both this row's fix and row 21 cycle 1's already-landed one): it is GREEN at the EXISTING pin `(136,20,11,9,36)`, unmoved. Shape (b) -- 27/168 `data/corpus/*/class/*.json` records with no `raw_tokens` -- FIXED, deferral closed.** Root cause confirmed: a structurally different, OLDER generator/schema (`gen_core_rulebook_cache.rs::class_cache_data` for `core_rulebook`, `cache_gen::acg`/`cache_gen::apg`'s equivalents) derives `class_id`/`maxlevel`/`bab`/`save_fort`/`save_ref`/`save_will` as Rust-side numeric-formula strings and never captured `raw_tokens` at all -- genuinely no fourth-mechanism rebuild needed, since every one of these 27 records already carries a real `source.kind:"lst_token"` citation (`path`+`line`+`record_key`). Built `src/bin/enrich_class_raw_tokens.rs`, the SAME additive, non-destructive, book-agnostic patch-in-place shape `enrich_equipment_raw_tokens.rs`/`enrich_spell_raw_tokens.rs` already established for this exact class of gap: re-reads the cited `.lst` line, unions in every `.MOD`/`.COPY=` row via `WiringClassIndex::closure_rows` (row 21's own machinery, reused not reinvented), PI-screens every field, and inserts ONLY `data.raw_tokens` -- every other field untouched by construction (raw `serde_json::Value` edit, never a typed re-serialize). Run for real: **27 of 27 enriched, 0 citation misses, 0 dropped for declared PI.** `git status --porcelain` showed exactly 27 modified files, matching the intended target exactly. Corrected via `scripts/retro.py correction --corrects row21-shape-b-class-raw-tokens-not-fixed` (dedupe-key `t9-onboarding-row21-cycle2-shape-b-closed`) -- the inherited deferral is closed, not left standing (`§27b`). **4. The 9 `pathfinder_unchained` records -- re-derived, decided, and FIXED in their owning generator.** Re-derived the exact 9 by cross-referencing every PU-owned (`data.class_key` present) `class_feature` record's own `key` against every real `.MOD` target in `pu_abilities_class.lst` (CATEGORY-prefix-stripped the same way `wiring_class::mod_base_name` already does): `Unchained {Summoner~Eidolon, Summoner~Summon Monster, Barbarian~Danger Sense, Barbarian~Rage Powers, Barbarian~Damage Reduction, Monk~Ki Powers, Monk~Flurry of Blows, Rogue~Finesse Training, Rogue~Rogue Talents}` -- an EXACT match to the 9 the row 21 cycle 1 receipt named. **Evidence they genuinely need it:** confirmed live for `Unchained Summoner ~ Eidolon` -- its shipped `raw_bonus_chains` carried exactly 1 entry (the base row's own `EidolonCompanionLVL|SummonerLVL`) while its real `.lst` closure carries **14** `.MOD`-appended rows, 8 with real `BONUS:VAR` content (the `EidolonEvolution` progression formula plus 10 `EidolonSubtype_*` selection bonuses) -- genuine, silently-dropped mechanical content, not inert tokens. Root cause: `ingest_pu_classes.rs`'s own `raw_tokens_excluding_bonus`/`raw_bonus_chains` took a single base `&LstRow`, inheriting the SAME single-row-read defect row 21 fixed for the generic path -- `class_feature::generate()`'s `foreign_citations` guard correctly refuses to overwrite a PU-owned coordinate (working as designed, not defeated), so the generic fix never reached these 9. **Fixed in the owning generator**, per the brief: `raw_tokens_excluding_bonus`/`raw_bonus_chains` now take a `&[LstRow]` closure, unioned across the base row plus every real `.MOD` row -- built via `wiring_index.closure_rows` (the SAME `WiringClassIndex` this file already constructs and already uses for `wiring_class_for`, reused not duplicated), at both this file's call sites (class-variant AND class-feature). **Near-miss caught before commit**: `ingest_pu_classes::main` unconditionally wipes the WHOLE `data/corpus/pathfinder_unchained/{class,class_feature}` trees before regenerating -- but `class_feature/` is SHARED with the generic pipeline's own 540 non-foreign records; a live run of the now-fixed `main()` deleted all 540, caught by `git status --porcelain` before any commit, reverted (`scripts/retro.py incident`, dedupe-key `t9-onboarding-ingest-pu-classes-wipe-near-miss`; fixing that separate directory-ownership defect is out of this row's scope, named for a follow-on). Applied the fix WITHOUT that destructive path instead: `src/bin/enrich_pu_class_feature_mod_closure.rs`, the same additive patch-in-place shape as item 3, scoped to exactly the 9 coordinates (cross-checked against `data.class_key` presence, never a directory sweep). Run for real: **exactly 9 of 604 `pathfinder_unchained` `class_feature` files patched, 55 already-correct left untouched, 540 non-foreign left untouched, 0 citation misses, 0 dropped for PI.** `git status --porcelain` confirms exactly 9 modified files. Stamp preservation proved BY FIELD: only `data.raw_tokens`/`data.raw_bonus_chains` changed on all 9, every other field byte-identical. **One stale pinned assertion swept**: `pathfinder_unchained::monk_features::tests::every_modelled_formula_is_byte_exact_against_the_ingested_corpus_record` asserted `Flurry of Blows`' `raw_bonus_chains` was empty, with a comment explicitly documenting the very `.MOD`-block-not-folded-in defect this fix closes -- updated to assert the real, now-restored 10-token content (cross-checked against the sibling `#[ignore]`d oracle-gated test's own independent pin of the same raw `.lst` rows, which now also passes). **A real double-skip content-loss bug in this cycle's own first-draft tools, caught before commit.** Both new enrichment tools' first draft called `tab_tokens(row).into_iter().skip(1)` -- `corpus_literal_sweep::tab_tokens` already performs its own `.skip(1)` (it documents "field 0 ... never a `KEY:VALUE` token"), so the extra skip silently dropped each record's FIRST real token (`core_rulebook/class/fighter.json` lost its own `HD:10`, across all 36 touched records on the first run). Caught by a manual field-list diff against the known raw `.lst` line order, before any commit; both corpus writes were reverted (`git checkout`), the bug fixed (single `.skip(1)`, matching `tab_tokens`'s own contract), and both tools re-run clean. Logged: `scripts/retro.py incident`, dedupe-key `t9-onboarding-tab-tokens-double-skip`. **PI screening, before and after (the gap row 21 named in its own evidence, closed here).** `declared_pi_shipping_audit`: run before this cycle's changes and after (full fix applied) -- both CLEAN, no shipped record contradicts its own corpus row's PI declaration. A scoped `pi_scrub.py::blacklist_term_hit_including_concatenated` scan (the SAME concatenated-identifier-aware scan `§24b` mandates) over every field of all 36 touched records' `raw_tokens`/`raw_bonus_chains`: **0 blacklist hits**, both directions. Content is plain PCGen mechanical tokens (`BONUS`, `DEFINE`, `TYPE`, `SOURCEPAGE`, `HD`, `MAXLEVEL`, `ROLE`, `FACT`) -- no `DESC`/name-shaped fields carried any real content in this population. **Sweep for stale pinned counts.** `apps/desktop/src-tauri`'s `class_feature_feat_bridge::{class_feature_feat_bridge_serves_the_full_corpus_wide_population, every_bridged_record_corpus_wide_carries_its_granted_feat}` were RED both before AND after this row's changes (`612` observed vs `613` pinned, byte-identical failure with the 27+9 files reverted to their pre-cycle-2 HEAD content) -- confirmed pre-existing drift, unrelated to this row (that module scans only `class_feature/`, never `class/`, and this row's 9 PU records did not change that count before OR after). Named here, not this row's territory to fix (a sibling lane's own pinned count, likely `row 19`'s "471 -> 613" note against a corpus that has since moved again). **`§16` numbers, kept distinct:** derived-evaluator-fixture-check bar 1 real defect FOUND and FIXED (RED->GREEN by mutation, reverted); class records carrying raw_tokens 141/168 -> **168/168**; pathfinder_unchained class_feature records with a correct `.MOD` closure 55/64 -> **64/64**; root scoped suites (`derived_evaluator_fixture_check` 5/5, `class_feature_grant_consumer` 28/28, `bonus_stack_reader` 21/21, `cache_gen::class_feature*` 87/87 incl. live-oracle-gated, `pilot_compute` 947/947, `wiring_class` 62/62, `ingest_pu_classes` 24/24, `class_feature_pool_catalog` 23/23): all green, 0 failed; `apps/desktop/src-tauri` (a SEPARATE cargo workspace): **536 passed / 2 failed** -- both pre-existing, confirmed unrelated by isolation re-run, was 538/0 before row 21 landed per the brief. Receipt: `artifacts/gate-3-closure-invariant/row21-cycle2-aftermath-closure_cycle-1_cycle_receipt.md`. |

**PI-over-redaction defect found and fixed (2026-08-23, t9-onboarding cycle, `sd32-pi-over-redaction-formulas`).** Root cause of the 83 `pi_redacted_formula` units: `scripts/pi_scrub.py::scrub_name_pi_tokens`'s needle generator split a `~`-delimited `KEY` segment into individual WORDS with no length floor, turning ordinary PCGen/Pathfinder vocabulary ("Trait", "Temp", "Bonus", "Evangelist", "Sentinel", "Exalted", ...) into standalone redaction needles — an UNRELATED, genuinely PI-free `BONUS`/`DEFINE` value got wiped to `[redacted PI]` merely for sharing a common word with a different part of the record's own (correctly, separately PI-blocked) name. Fixed by (1) gating BOTH needle sets on the same `_MIN_NORMALIZED_NEEDLE_LEN` floor, and (2) removing the per-WORD split entirely (segment/name/key-level matching only) — this alone fixes the whole class while leaving every genuine self-reference and every blacklist-term concatenation catch (checks 1/3/4) untouched, proved by 3 new mutation-proof tests in `scripts/tests/test_pi_scrub.py` (13/13 green) plus the 4 dependent PI suites (101 tests total, all green). Re-derivation split the 83: **63 recovered** (genuinely PI-free, formula restored), **20 correctly remain redacted** (7 records where the record's own FULL name/key segment appears verbatim in the value — e.g. an `ABILITYPOOL` restating the record's own pool name — and 13 where a real blacklist term (6 distinct terms across the 13 records, never named here per `§24b`-2) is concatenated into the value, both genuine PI needing no fix). Regenerated the 68 affected corpus files (`scripts/regen_row17_pi_over_redaction.py`, scoped to exactly the row-17 `fallthrough`+`pi_redacted_formula` population, never the 856-record corpus-wide `regen_all_renamed_pi_scrub.py` blast radius, to stay clear of sibling-lane territory) through a pipeline that mirrors `ingest_generic_kind.py::remediate`'s full sequence (declared-PI detection → DESC blanking → blacklist scan → identity/blacklist scan), not `scrub_name_pi_tokens` alone — a first draft that skipped the DESC-blanking step was caught live by `cargo run --bin declared_pi_shipping_audit` (3 new `DESC-PI-SHIPPED-IN-RAW-TOKENS` violations, corpus reverted and the script fixed before any commit; the same gap was confirmed to also be latent, pre-existing, and unfixed in `regen_all_renamed_pi_scrub.py` itself — reported, not silently patched there, since that driver is broader corpus-wide territory). **PI audits, before vs. after (`§12c`):** `python3 scripts/pi_key_rawtokens_audit.py` — confirmed 0 → 0 (unchanged; candidate/informational bucket shifts only, as expected from restored generic-word formula text); `cargo run --bin declared_pi_shipping_audit` — 65 → 65, all still in `bestiary_4/monster_ability` (sibling territory, zero new violations, zero in any of the 68 touched files). Row-17 honest size: **84 → 21** (fallthrough 21, of which pi_redacted 20 — the 7+13 genuine cases above). The 84th (non-PI) unit, `ultimate_campaign:trait:trait_harvester` (`BONUS:SKILL|%LIST`, a 2-part PCGen shorthand `extract_formula_segment` doesn't have a magnitude segment for — `%LIST` substitutes the CHOOSE'd skill but the token carries no explicit numeric value to classify), is a separate, single-unit, non-PI classifier gap, named here for a follow-up cycle rather than guessed at. Receipt: `artifacts/gate-1-shape-closure/t9-onboarding-pi-over-redaction_cycle-1_cycle_receipt.md`. |

**Row 17's remaining 21 closed to ZERO (2026-08-23, t9-onboarding, `sd32-row17-remaining-21`).** Re-derived fresh (`§17a`): `python3 scripts/row17_census.py` confirmed the inherited 21 (`no_record` 106, all sibling-owned; `row17_honest_size` 21 = fallthrough 21, of which 20 PI-redacted formula + the 1 named `trait_harvester` classifier gap). Three fixes: **(1) narrower redaction, not just relabeling.** `pi_scrub.py::scrub_name_pi_tokens` gained an opt-in `neutral_name` parameter: when a value's ONLY hit is a plain, space-preserving self-reference to the record's own (already-`§24`-renamed) name/key — never a blacklist term — it substitutes ONLY the matched span with the record's own coordinate-derived neutral name, preserving the surrounding mechanical structure (magnitude, `TYPE=`, `PRECLASS=`, ...) instead of wiping the whole token; a value that also hits the blacklist is never narrowed. Applied via `regen_row17_pi_over_redaction.py` (now threading each record's own `codex_neutral_name`): **6 of the 20 PI-redacted units recovered real formula content** and moved OUT of F0 into a real family (`derived` 11402→11408) — a genuine closure, not a relabeling (`§16`). Zero new PI leaks: `pi_key_rawtokens_audit.py` confirmed 0→0 both before and after (population 27,538/26,768→27,538 scanned, one-record shift already explained by `§26`'s unrelated fix), `declared_pi_shipping_audit` 65→65 unchanged (all pre-existing `bestiary_4/monster_ability`, sibling territory, zero of the 6 touched files), diff-scanned against all 61 `PI_BLACKLIST_TERMS` and the 7 records' own original PI names — zero matches in any added line. **(2) classification fix for the remaining 14 genuinely-PI-redacted units.** `shape_ledger.py::classify_unit` now emits a distinct `f0_reached_by == "measured_pi_redacted"` (a real, measured answer — "PI, cannot ship as formula") instead of lumping a genuinely-redacted value into `fallthrough` (the placeholder bucket) — per `§27a`: *"if the value genuinely carries PI, it stays redacted — but then it is not a fallthrough placeholder, it is a correctly-measured redacted value."* `row17_census.py` reports it as its own line item, excluded from `row17_honest_size` the same way `measured_empty` already is. Mutation-proved (RED→GREEN) in both `test_shape_ledger.py` and `test_row17_census.py`. **(3) the 21st unit, `ultimate_campaign:trait:trait_harvester`'s `BONUS:SKILL|%LIST`.** Confirmed against the pinned oracle: this exact 2-field shorthand (no magnitude field) is the ONLY occurrence anywhere in the whole corpus (`grep`-verified across every `.lst` file); the record's own `ASPECT`/`DESC` text ("+1 trait bonus...") confirms the omitted magnitude is a flat +1 (Pathfinder's universal trait-skill-bonus convention). `extract_formula_segment` extended with a narrowly-scoped rule (2-field `BONUS:SKILL|%LIST` → implicit `"1"`; a bare `SKILL|<real skill name>` with no magnitude stays the genuine parse failure it already was) — generic in implementation even though the current population it serves is 1, per `§17`. **Result: `ROW 17 HONEST SIZE: 21 → 0`** (`fallthrough` 0, `measured_pi_redacted` 14, `not_ingested` 106 unchanged/sibling-owned). **Discovery-forward, fixed in this cycle (not left for a successor): the SAME DESC-blanking gap `regen_row17_pi_over_redaction.py`'s own module docstring named as still-latent in its two siblings was confirmed present in BOTH `regen_all_renamed_pi_scrub.py` and `regen_generic_kind_pi_scrub.py`** (identical shape: `row_tokens` + `scrub_name_pi_tokens` alone, omitting DESC pre-blanking, would ship un-redacted `DESCISPI:YES` prose in `raw_tokens` whenever the prose doesn't happen to restate the record's own name). Fixed by having both import and call the SAME `regen_row17_pi_over_redaction.redact_tokens` (not a fourth divergent copy), with a new `test_regen_scripts_desc_blanking_parity.py` (4 tests: DESC-blanking behavior, a mutation-proof reproducing the OLD leak, and import-identity checks on both siblings so a future regression back to a local copy fails at import time). **Neither corpus-wide script was RUN against the live corpus** — both dry-run cleanly (846/1,992 records scanned) but their populations (`equipment`/`ability`/`deity`/`monster`/...) are sibling-lane territory per this cycle's own scope note; only the code fix and tests land here. Still `backlog` (unchanged — `no_record` 106 is a sibling lane's ingest work; row 17 itself cannot start until that reaches zero, `§27`/`§20`). 264 total tests run across all touched suites, all green (`scripts/tests/test_pi_scrub.py` 17, `test_shape_ledger.py` 62, `test_row17_census.py` 6, `test_regen_scripts_desc_blanking_parity.py` 4, plus 173+2 dependent PI/regen suites unchanged and still green). Receipt: `artifacts/gate-1-shape-closure/t9-onboarding-row17-remaining-21_cycle-1_cycle_receipt.md`.

## Status values

- `pending` — card exists, no cycle has claimed it.
- `in-progress` — a cycle has claimed it; cycle receipt pending.
- `complete` — cycle receipt is in `progress.md` and the gate's AT-32-* criteria are met.
- `returned-to-backlog` — cycle ran, hit a non-self-healable blocker; receipt filed under
  `## Open blockers` in `progress.md`.
- `DISCOVERED-forked` — cycle found work that didn't fit the card; new card opened in the
  discovery row above (or in `forward-scope-register.md` if it doesn't fit SD-32).

## Cross-SD gate discipline

There are no cross-SD gates for SD-32 to cite at launch (no sibling bundle is the precondition;
the dependency on SD-31 is its content being merged to develop — PR #374, 2026-08-22, verified by
content in `workflow-instruction.md §1` item 3 rather than by a per-cycle PI-screen citation). The PI-gate and
declared-PI reader are not consumed here because SD-32 does not produce records that ship into
the player-facing app in its own right — the engines it builds are surfaced via
`reach_gate.rs` from the existing ingest path that already carries the PI-gate.

## Why no Hermes kanban board

The Hermes kanban board was retired 2026-08-01 (`SD-30-class-feature-archetype-bundle/decisions.md`
Decision 14a); SD-30 and its successors use the local-file pattern. This is documented at
`workflow-instruction.md §1` item 1, `workflow-instruction.md §2`, and `workflow-instruction.md §6` step 8.
